we apply the first bound (i) in
Lemma
3
(with
\mu=\nu
) and use
Definition
2
:
\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}=\min_{\nu\,\in\,\Delta({\mathcal{S}})}\max_{\pi\,\in\,\Pi}\|d^{\pi}_{\rho}/\nu\|_{\infty}
, and in
(b)
we use
|\Phi^{\pi}(\nu)-\Phi^{\pi^{\prime}}(\nu)|\leq C_{\Phi}
for any
\pi,\pi^{\prime}
, and further simplify the bound in
(b)
.
We complete the proof for the first bound by taking stepsize
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{2.5}\sqrt{C_{\Phi}}}{NA\sqrt{T}}
(by the upper bound of
C_{\Phi}
given in
Lemma
18
, the condition
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{\sqrt{A}}
is satisfied).

If we proceed
(
17
)
with the second bound (ii) in
Lemma
3
with the choice of
\eta\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{8\kappa_{\nu}^{3}NA}
, then,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V_{i}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\\
&\leq&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\sup_{\pi\,\in\,\Pi}\|d_{\rho}^{\pi}/\nu\|_{\infty}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{T}\times\sqrt{4\eta(1-\gamma)\left(\Phi^{(T+1)}(\nu)-\Phi^{(1)}(\nu)\right)}\\[11.38092pt]
&\lesssim&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\sup_{\pi\,\in\,\Pi}\|d_{\rho}^{\pi}/\nu\|_{\infty}TC_{\Phi}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}}}.\end{array}

We next discuss two special choices of
\nu
for proving our bound. First, if
\nu=\rho
, then
\eta\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{8\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}
. By letting
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{8\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}
, the last square root term can be bounded by
O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{4}NATC_{\Phi}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}}\right)
. Second, if
\nu=\frac{1}{S}\mathbf{1}
, the uniform distribution over
{\mathcal{S}}
, then
\kappa_{\nu}\leq\frac{1}{S}
, which allows a valid choice
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{8S^{3}NA}\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{8\kappa_{\nu}^{3}NA}
. Hence, we can bound the last square root term by
O\left(\sqrt{\frac{S^{4}NATC_{\Phi}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}}}\right)
. Since
\nu
is arbitrary, combining these two special choices completes the proof.
∎

B.2
Proof of
Theorem
2

We first establish policy improvement regarding the
Q
-function at two consecutive policies
\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
1
.

Lemma 5

(Policy improvement: Markov cooperative games)
.

For MPG
(
1
)
with identical rewards and an initial state distribution
\rho>0
, if all players independently perform the policy update in
Algorithm
1
with stepsize
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{2N}
, then for any
t
and any
s
,

\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\,-\,\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\;\geq\;\frac{1}{4\eta}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot|s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot|s)}\right\|^{2}

where
\eta
is the stepsize and
N
is the number of players.

Proof of
Lemma
5
.

Fixing the time
t
and the state
s
, we apply
Lemma
2
to

\displaystyle\Psi^{\pi}\;=\;\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]

where
Q^{(t)}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=Q^{\pi^{(t)}}
(recall that
\pi
is a joint policy of all players). By
Lemma
2
, for any two policies
\pi^{\prime}
and
\pi
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\,-\,\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\left(\mathbb{E}_{a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{-i}\,\sim\,\pi_{-i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi^{\prime}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{j}\,\sim\,\pi_{j}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&&\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\displaystyle-\,\mathbb{E}_{a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{j}\,\sim\,\pi_{j}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&&\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\displaystyle-\,\mathbb{E}_{a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi^{\prime}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{j}\,\sim\,\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&&\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\displaystyle+\,\mathbb{E}_{a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{j}\,\sim\,\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\Bigg)\end{array}

(18)

where
\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}
is a joint policy of players
N\backslash\{i,j\}
in which players
<i
and
i\sim j
use
\pi
, and players
>j
use
\pi^{\prime}
. Particularly, we choose
\pi^{\prime}=\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi=\pi^{(t)}
. Thus, we can reduce
(
18
)
into

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\,-\,\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i}}\left(\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i},\,a_{j}}\left(\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\left(\pi^{\prime}_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)\right)\mathbb{E}_{a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i},\,a_{j}}\left|\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right|\left|\pi^{\prime}_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)\right|\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}-\frac{A}{2(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\left(\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\pi^{\prime}_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}-\frac{(N-1)A}{2(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(c)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{4\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\end{array}

where
(a)
is due to the optimality condition
(
14
)
and
Q^{(t)}(s,a)\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,
(b)
is due to
\langle x,y\rangle\leq\frac{\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}}{2}
, and
(c)
follows the choice of
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{2NA}
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
2
.

By
Lemma
1
and
Lemma
5
, we have for any
\nu\in\Delta({\mathcal{S}})
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}V^{(t+1)}(\nu)\,-\,V^{(t)}(\nu)&=&\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,\,a}d_{\nu}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left(\pi^{(t+1)}(a\,|\,s)-\pi^{(t)}(a\,|\,s)\right)Q^{(t)}(s,a)\\[5.69046pt]
&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\nu}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}.\end{array}

(19)

By the same argument as the proof of
Theorem
1
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{3}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}\sqrt{d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\times d_{\nu}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)}\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|\\[14.22636pt]
&\leq&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\nu}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}}\\[14.22636pt]
&\overset{(c)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\nu}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}}\\[17.07182pt]
&\overset{(d)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{T}\times\sqrt{4\eta(1-\gamma)\left(V^{(T+1)}(\nu)-V^{(1)}(\nu)\right)}\end{array}

where in
(a)
we slightly abuse the notation
i
to represent
\argmax_{i}
as in
(
17
)
, in
(b)
we take
\nu=\argmin_{\nu\in\Delta({\mathcal{S}})}\max_{\pi\,\in\,\Pi}\|d_{\rho}^{\pi}/\nu\|_{\infty}
and use the definition of
\widetilde{\kappa}_{\rho}
from
Definition
2
, and we replace
i
(
\argmax_{i}
in
(a)
) in the last square root term in
(c)
by the sum over all players, and we apply
(
19
)
in
(d)
.

Finally, we complete the proof by taking stepsize
\eta=\frac{1-\gamma}{2NA}
and using
V^{(T+1)}(\nu)-V^{(1)}(\nu)\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.
∎

Appendix C
Proofs for Section
5

In this section, we provide proofs of
Theorem
3
and
Theorem
4
in
Appendix
C.2
and
Appendix
C.3
, respectively.

C.1
Unbiased estimate

We consider the
k
th sampling in the data collection phase of
Algorithm
2
. By the sampling model in lines 6-8 of
Algorithm
2
, it is straightforward to see that
\bar{s}^{(h_{i})}\sim d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}
for player
i
. Then, we take
\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{i}^{(k)}(\cdot\,|\,s^{(h_{i})})
at step
h_{i}
for player
i
. Each player
i
begins with such
(\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})})
while all players execute the policy
\{\pi_{i}^{(t)}\}_{i\,=\,1}^{N}
with the termination probability
1-\gamma
. Once terminated, we add all rewards collected in
R_{i}^{(k)}
. We next show that
\mathbb{E}[\,R_{i}^{(k)}\,]=\bar{Q}_{i}^{\pi}(\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})})
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbb{E}[\,R_{i}^{(k)}\,]&=&\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\sum_{h\,=\,h_{i}}^{h_{i}+h_{i}^{\prime}-1}\bar{r}_{i}^{(h)}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})}),h_{i}^{\prime}\sim\textsc{Geometric}(1-\gamma)\,\right]\\[14.22636pt]
&\overset{(a)}{=}&\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\sum_{h\,=\,0}^{h_{i}^{\prime}-1}\bar{r}_{i}^{(h+h_{i})}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})}),h_{i}^{\prime}\sim\textsc{Geometric}(1-\gamma)\,\right]\\[14.22636pt]
&=&\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\sum_{h\,=\,0}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{1}_{\{0\,\leq\,h\,\leq h_{i}^{\prime}-1\}}\bar{r}_{i}^{(h+h_{i})}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})}),h_{i}^{\prime}\sim\textsc{Geometric}(1-\gamma)\,\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(b)}{=}&\displaystyle\sum_{h\,=\,0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\,\mathbb{E}_{h_{i}^{\prime}}\big[\boldsymbol{1}_{\{0\,\leq\,h\,\leq h_{i}^{\prime}-1\}}\big]\,\bar{r}_{i}^{(h+h_{i})}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})})\,\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(c)}{=}&\displaystyle\sum_{h\,=\,0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\,\gamma^{h}\,\bar{r}_{i}^{(h+h_{i})}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})})\,\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&=&\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\,\sim\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})})}\mathbb{E}\left[\,\sum_{h\,=\,0}^{\infty}\gamma^{h}\,\bar{r}_{i}^{(h+h_{i})}\,\bigg|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\sim\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})})\,\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&=&\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})}\,\sim\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,\bar{s}^{(h_{i})})}\left[Q_{i}^{(t)}(\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{-i}^{(h_{i})})\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(\bar{s}^{(h_{i})},\bar{a}_{i}^{(h_{i})})\end{array}

where in
(a)
we change the range of index
h
while using the same initial state and action,
(b)
is due to the tower property,
(c)
follows that
\mathbb{E}_{h_{i}^{\prime}}\big[\boldsymbol{1}_{\{0\,\leq\,h\,\leq h_{i}^{\prime}-1\}}\big]=1-(1-(1-p)^{h})=\gamma^{h}
, where
p=1-\gamma
, and we also apply the monotone convergence and dominated convergence theorems for swapping the sum and the expectation.

C.2
Proof of
Theorem
3

We apply
Lemma
2
to the potential function
\Phi^{\pi}(\rho)
at two consecutive policies
\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
2
, where
\rho
is the initial state distribution. We use the shorthand
\Phi^{(t)}(\rho)
for
\Phi^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)
, the value of potential function at policy
\pi^{(t)}
. The proof extends
Lemma
3
by accounting for the statistical error in
Assumption
2
.

Lemma 6

(Policy improvement: Markov potential games)
.

Let
Assumption
1
hold.
In
Algorithm
2
, the potential function
\Phi^{\pi}(\rho)
at two consecutive policies
\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi^{(t)}
satisfies

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\text{(i)}\;\;\displaystyle\Phi^{(t+1)}(\rho)-\Phi^{(t)}(\rho)&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi^{(t)}_{-i}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}-\,\frac{4\eta^{2}AW^{2}N^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\frac{\eta\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)^{2}\xi}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}\\
\text{(ii)}\;\;\displaystyle\Phi^{(t+1)}(\rho)-\Phi^{(t)}(\rho)&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi^{(t)}_{-i}}(s)\left(1-{\frac{4\eta\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}}\right)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{\eta\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)^{2}\xi}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}\end{array}

where
\eta
is the stepsize,
N
is the number of players,
A
is the size of one player’s action space,
W
is the 2-norm bound of
\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}
, and
\kappa_{\rho}
is the distribution mismatch coefficient relative to
\rho
(see
\kappa_{\rho}
in
Definition
1
).

Proof of
Lemma
6
.

We let
\pi^{\prime}=\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi=\pi^{(t)}
for brevity. We first express

\Phi^{(t+1)}(\rho)-\Phi^{(t)}(\rho)=\textbf{Diff}_{\alpha}+\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}
, where
\textbf{Diff}_{\alpha}
and
\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}
are given as those in
(
12
)
.

Bounding

\textbf{Diff}_{\alpha}
. By the property of the potential function
\Phi^{\pi}(\rho)
and
Lemma
1
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Phi^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(\rho)\,-\,\Phi^{\pi}(\rho)&=&V_{i}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(\rho)\,-\,V_{i}^{\pi}(\rho)\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,\,a_{i}}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(s)\left(\pi_{i}^{\prime}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\bar{Q}_{i}^{\pi_{i},\,\pi_{-i}}(s,a_{i}).\end{array}

The optimality of
\pi_{i}^{\prime}=\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}
in line 14 of
Algorithm
2
leads to

\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s),\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\big\|\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)\big\|^{2}\;\geq\;\big\langle\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}.

(20)

Hence,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Phi^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(\rho)\,-\,\Phi^{\pi}(\rho)&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}}(s)\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s),\bar{Q}_{i}^{\pi_{i},\pi_{-i}}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}.\end{array}

Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\textbf{Diff}_{\alpha}&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\big\langle\big(\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}-\pi_{i}^{(t)}\big)(\cdot\,|\,s),\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}.\end{array}

However,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\big\langle\big(\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}-\pi_{i}^{(t)}\big)(\cdot\,|\,s),\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\geq}&\displaystyle-\,\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left(\frac{1}{2\eta^{\prime}}\left\|{\big(\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}-\pi_{i}^{(t)}\big)(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{\eta^{\prime}}{2}\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{=}&\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4\eta}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\big(\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}-\pi_{i}^{(t)}\big)(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\eta\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\end{array}

where
(a)
follows the inequality
\langle x,y\rangle\leq\frac{\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}}{2\eta^{\prime}}+\frac{\eta^{\prime}\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}}{2}
for
\eta^{\prime}>0
, and we choose
\eta^{\prime}=2\eta
in
(b)
.

Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\textbf{Diff}_{\alpha}&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{\eta}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}.\end{array}

(21)

Bounding

\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}
. For simplicity, we denote
\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}
as the joint policy of players
N\backslash\{i,j\}
where players
<i
and
i\sim j
use
\pi
and players
>j
use
\pi^{\prime}
. As done in the proof of
Lemma
3
, we can bound each summand in
\text{Diff}_{\beta}
except for the last step from
(c)
to
(d)
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\Phi^{\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij},\,\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{j}^{\prime}}(\rho)\,-\,\Phi^{\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij},\,\pi_{i},\,\pi_{j}^{\prime}}(\rho)\,-\,\Phi^{\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij},\,\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{j}}(\rho)\,+\,\Phi^{\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij},\,\pi_{i},\,\pi_{j}}(\rho)\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(c)}{\geq}&\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\max_{s}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|_{1}\right)\left(\max_{s}\left\|{\pi_{j}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|_{1}\right)\\[11.38092pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left(\max_{s}\left\|{\pi_{j}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|_{1}\right)\left(\max_{s}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|_{1}\right)\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(d)}{\geq}&\displaystyle-\,\frac{8\eta^{2}AW^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\end{array}

where
(d)
follows a direct result from the optimality of
\pi_{j}^{(t+1)}
given by
(
20
)
,

\left\|{\pi_{j}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|\;\leq\;2\eta\left\|{\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|\;\leq\;2\eta\,W

and that
\|\cdot\|_{1}\leq\sqrt{A}\|\cdot\|
.
Therefore,

\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}\;\geq\;-\,\frac{4\eta^{2}AW^{2}N^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}.

(22)

We now complete the proof of (i) by combining
(
21
)
and
(
22
)
and we also employ that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}{L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}\end{array}

where
(a)
follows the definition of
\kappa_{\rho}
and
(b)
is the definition of
L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})
:

\displaystyle L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\;\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\;\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\,d_{\rho}^{(t)},a_{i}\sim\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot|s)}\left[\big(\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\big)^{2}\right]\;\geq\;\frac{\xi}{A}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\rho}^{(t)}}\sum_{a_{i}}\big(\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\big)^{2}.

Alternatively, as done in
Lemma
3
, we can apply
Lemma
21
to each summand of
\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}
and show that

\textbf{Diff}_{\beta}\;\geq\;-\,\frac{2\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}.

Combining the inequality above with
(
21
)
finishes the proof of (ii).
∎

Proof of
Theorem
3
.

By the optimality of
\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}
in line 14 of
Algorithm
2
,

\Big\langle(1-\xi)\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)+\frac{\xi}{A}\boldsymbol{1}-\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\eta\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\pi^{(t+1)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)+\pi^{(t)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)\Big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\;\leq\;0,\;\text{ for any }\pi_{i}^{\prime}\,\in\,\Pi_{i}.

which leads to

\displaystyle\Big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\eta\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\Big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\;\Big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot|s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot|s)\Big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}

(23)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,\frac{\xi}{1-\xi}\Big\langle\pi^{(t+1)}(\cdot|s)-\frac{1}{A}\boldsymbol{1},\eta\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\pi^{(t+1)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)+\pi^{(t)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\;\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot|s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot|s)}\right\|\,+\,\eta\xi W

(24)

where the last inequality is because of
\|{\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\|\leq W
and
\xi\leq\frac{1}{2}
.
Hence, if
\eta\leq\frac{1}{W}
, then for any
\pi_{i}^{\prime}\in\Pi_{i}
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\,+\,\big\langle\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|{\pi^{(t+1)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi^{(t)}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|\,+\,\xi W\,+\,\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|\left\|{\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|\,+\,\xi W\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\end{array}

where we apply
(
24
)
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in
(a)
, and
(b)
is because
\|{\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\|\leq W
and
\eta\leq\frac{1}{W}
. As done in the proof of
Theorem
1
, the different steps begin from
(b)
in
(
17
)
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V_{i}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|+\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(c)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}\sqrt{d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\times d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|+\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\left|\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s)\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\right|\\[14.22636pt]
&\overset{(d)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{AL_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}{\xi}}\\[14.22636pt]
&\overset{(e)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{AL_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}{\xi}}\end{array}

(25)

where we slightly abuse the notation
\pi_{i}^{\prime}
in
(b)
to represent
\argmax_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}
and
i
represents
\argmax_{i}
as in
(
17
)
,
(c)
is due to the definition of the distribution mismatch coefficient (see it in
Definition
1
):

\frac{d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}{d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\;\leq\;\frac{d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}{(1-\gamma)\rho(s)}\;\leq\;\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma},

(d)
follows the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the inequality
\sqrt{\sum_{i}x_{i}}\leq\sum_{i}\sqrt{x_{i}}
for any
x_{i}\geq 0
, the Jensen’s inequality, and the definition of
L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\left|\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s)\big\langle\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s),\,\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\big\rangle_{{\mathcal{A}}_{i}}\right|\\[5.69046pt]
&\lesssim&\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s)}\sqrt{\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s)\sum_{a_{i}}\left(\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\right)^{2}}\\[5.69046pt]
&\leq&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{AL_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}{\xi}}\end{array}

where
L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\mathbb{E}_{s\,\sim\,d_{\rho}^{(t)},a\sim\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot|s)}\big[\big(\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\big)^{2}\big]
, and
\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})=\langle\phi_{i}(s,a_{i}),\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}\rangle
, and we replace
i
(
\argmax_{i}
in
(b)
) in the square root term in
(e)
by the sum over all players.

If we proceed
(
25
)
with the first bound (i) in
Lemma
6
, then,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V_{i}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\eta(1-\gamma)(\Phi^{(N+1)}-\Phi^{(1)})+\frac{\eta^{3}AW^{2}N^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}T+\frac{\eta^{2}\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{A\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}{\xi}}\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{\rho}TC_{\Phi}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}}}\,+\,T\sqrt{\frac{\eta\kappa_{\rho}AW^{2}N^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}\,T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{AN\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}{\xi}}\,+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\end{array}

where we apply the first bound (i) in
Lemma
6
and the telescoping sum for
(a)
, and we use the boundedness of the potential function:
|\Phi^{\pi}-\Phi^{\pi^{\prime}}|\leq C_{\Phi}
for any
\pi
and
\pi^{\prime}
, and further simplify the bound in
(f)
by Assumption
Assumption
2
.
We complete the proof of (i) by taking stepsize
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}\sqrt{C_{\Phi}}}{WN\sqrt{AT}}
and exploration rate

\xi\leq\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}NA\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}W^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}
.

If we proceed
(
25
)
with the first bound (ii) in
Lemma
6
with the choice of
\eta\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{16\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}
, then,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V_{i}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\eta(1-\gamma)(\Phi^{(N+1)}-\Phi^{(1)})+\frac{\eta^{2}\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{A\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}{\xi}}\\[11.38092pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\kappa_{\rho}TC_{\Phi}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}}}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}\,T}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{AN\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}{\xi}}\,+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\end{array}

which completes the proof if we choose
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{16\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}
and exploration rate

\xi\leq\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}NA\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}W^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}
.
∎

C.3
Proof of
Theorem
4

We first establish policy improvement regarding the
Q
-function at two consecutive policies
\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
2
.

Lemma 7

(Policy improvement: Markov cooperative games)
.

For MPG
(
1
)
with identical rewards and an initial state distribution
\rho>0
, if all players independently perform the policy update in
Algorithm
2
with stepsize
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{2N}
, then for any
t
and any
s
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\,-\,\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\pi^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\eta\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\left\|{{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{\bar{Q}}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\end{array}

where
\eta
is the stepsize and
N
is the number of players,

Proof of
Lemma
7
.

As done in the proof of
Lemma
5
, we let
\Psi^{\pi}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]
and
(
18
)
holds, where
Q^{(t)}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=Q^{\pi^{(t)}}
. By taking
\pi^{\prime}=\pi^{(t+1)}
and
\pi=\pi^{(t)}
for
(
18
)
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\,-\,\mathbb{E}_{a\,\sim\,\pi(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i}}\left(\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\,+\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i}}\left(\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\left(\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,a_{i})\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i},\,a_{j}}\left(\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right)\left(\pi^{\prime}_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)\right)\mathbb{E}_{a_{-ij}\,\sim\,\widetilde{\pi}_{-ij}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[Q^{(t)}(s,a)\right]\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\left(\frac{1}{2\eta^{\prime}}\left\|{\pi^{\prime}_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta^{\prime}}{2}\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\sum_{a_{i},\,a_{j}}\left|\pi^{\prime}_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(a_{i}\,|\,s)\right|\left|\pi^{\prime}_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(a_{j}\,|\,s)\right|\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{4\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\eta\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{j\,=\,i+1}^{N}\left(\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\pi^{\prime}_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{j}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\right)\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{1}{4\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\eta\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{N-1}{2(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(c)}{\geq}&\displaystyle\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\frac{1}{8\eta}\left\|{\pi_{i}^{\prime}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\eta\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\end{array}

where
(a)
is due to the optimality condition
(
20
)
, the inequality
\langle x,y\rangle\leq\frac{\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}}{2\eta^{\prime}}+\frac{\eta^{\prime}\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}}{2}
for
\eta^{\prime}>0
, and
Q^{(t)}(s,a)\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
,
(b)
is due to
\langle x,y\rangle\leq\frac{\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}}{2}
and
\eta^{\prime}=2\eta
, and
(c)
follows the choice of
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{4N}
.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
4
.

By
Lemma
1
and
Lemma
7
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}V^{(t+1)}(\rho)\,-\,V^{(t)}(\rho)&=&\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,\,a}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left(\pi^{(t+1)}(a\,|\,s)-\pi^{(t)}(a\,|\,s)\right)Q^{(t)}(s,a)\\[5.69046pt]
&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle-\,\frac{\eta}{1-\gamma}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\geq&\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|{\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{\eta\kappa_{\rho}A}{\xi(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}\end{array}

where the last inequality is due to that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{\bar{Q}_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s,\cdot)-\widehat{Q}_{i}^{\pi^{(t)}}(s,\cdot)}\right\|^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{{(t)}}(s)\sum_{a_{i}}\frac{\xi}{A}\big(\bar{Q}_{i}^{{(t)}}(s,a_{i})-\langle\phi_{i}(s,a_{i}),\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}\rangle\big)^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}{\,\mathbb{E}_{s\,\sim\,d_{\rho}^{{(t)}},\,a_{i}\,\sim\,\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[\big(\bar{Q}_{i}^{{(t)}}(s,a_{i})-\langle\phi_{i}(s,a_{i}),\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}\rangle\big)^{2}\right]}\\[11.38092pt]
&=&\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}{L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}\end{array}

where
(a)
follows the definition of
\kappa_{\rho}
and
(b)
is the definition of
L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})
.

By the same argument as the proof of
Theorem
3
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\\[11.38092pt]
&\lesssim&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{\pi^{(t+1)}}(s)\left\|\pi_{i}^{(t+1)}(\cdot\,|\,s)-\pi_{i}^{(t)}(\cdot\,|\,s)\right\|^{2}}\\[11.38092pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{AL_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})}{\xi}}.\end{array}

By taking expectation and the Jensen’s inequality,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{i}\left(\max_{\pi_{i}^{\prime}}V^{\pi_{i}^{\prime},\,\pi_{-i}^{(t)}}(\rho)-V^{\pi^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)\right]\\[11.38092pt]
&\lesssim&\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}}}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{8\eta(1-\gamma)(V^{(N+1)}-V^{(1)})+\frac{8\eta^{2}\kappa_{\rho}A}{(1-\gamma)\xi}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{N}{\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}}\\[11.38092pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\kappa_{\rho}}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{A\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]}{\xi}}\\[11.38092pt]
&\lesssim&\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{8\kappa_{\rho}T}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{3}}}\,+\,{\kappa_{\rho}}{T}\sqrt{\frac{8AN}{(1-\gamma)^{4}\xi}{\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}}\,+\,\frac{\xi TW}{1-\gamma}.\end{array}

We complete the proof by taking stepsize
\eta=\frac{1-\gamma}{2NA}
, exploration rate

\xi\leq\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}NA\epsilon_{\text{stat}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}W^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}
, and using
V^{(N+1)}-V^{(1)}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
.
∎

C.4
Sample complexity

We present our sample complexity guarantees for
Algorithm
2
in which the regression problem
(
7
)
in each iteration is approximately solved by the stochastic projected gradient descent
(
38
)
. We measure the sample complexity by the total number of trajectory samples
TK
, where
T
is the number of iterations and
K
is the batch size of trajectories.

Corollary 1

(Sample complexity for Markov potential games)
.

Assume the setting in
Theorem
3
except for
Assumption
2
.
Suppose we compute
\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k\,=\,1}^{K}\beta_{k}^{(K)}{w}_{i}^{(k)}
via a stochastic projected gradient descent
(
38
)
with stepsize
\lambda^{(k)}=\frac{2}{2+k}
and
\beta_{k}^{(K)}=\frac{1/\lambda^{(k)}}{\sum_{r\,=\,1}^{K}1/\lambda^{(r)}}
. Then, if we choose stepsize
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{3/2}\sqrt{C_{\Phi}}}{WN\sqrt{AT}}
and exploration rate
\xi=\min\left(\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}ANd}{(1-\gamma)^{4}K}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},\frac{1}{2}\right)
, then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\text{Nash-Regret}(T)\,\right]\;\lesssim\;\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}\,WN}(\,AC_{\Phi}\,)^{\frac{1}{4}}}{(1-\gamma)^{\frac{7}{2}}\,T^{\frac{1}{4}}}\,+\,\frac{W(\,{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}\,ANd}\,)^{\frac{1}{3}}}{(1-\gamma)^{\frac{7}{3}}\,K^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

Furthermore, if we choose stepsize
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}}{16\kappa_{\rho}^{3}NA}
and exploration rate
\xi=\min\left(\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}ANd}{(1-\gamma)^{4}K}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},\frac{1}{2}\right)
, then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\text{Nash-Regret}(T)\,\right]\;\lesssim\;\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}\,\sqrt{ANC_{\Phi}}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}\sqrt{T}}\,+\,\frac{W(\,{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}\,ANd}\,)^{\frac{1}{3}}}{(1-\gamma)^{\frac{7}{3}}\,K^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

Moreover, their sample complexity guarantees are
TK=O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{7}})
or
TK=O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{5}})
, respectively, for obtaining an
\epsilon
-Nash equilibrium.

Proof of
Corollary
1
.

By the unbiased estimate in
Appendix
C.1
, the stochastic gradient
\widehat{\nabla}_{i}^{(t)}
in
(
38
)
is also unbiased. We note the variance of the stochastic gradient is bounded by
\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
. By
Lemma
23
, if we choose
\lambda^{(k)}=\frac{2}{2+k}
and
\beta_{k}^{(K)}=\frac{1/\lambda^{(k)}}{\sum_{r\,=\,1}^{K}1/\lambda^{(r)}}
, then

\mathbb{E}\left[L_{i}^{(t)}(\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)})\right]\,-\,L_{i}^{(t)}(w_{i}^{(t)})\;\leq\;\frac{dW^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}K}

where
L_{i}^{(t)}(w_{i}^{(t)})=0
. by
Assumption
1
. Therefore, substitution of
\epsilon_{\text{stat}}\leq\frac{dW^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}K}
into
Theorem
3
yields desired results.

Finally, we let the upper bound on
\text{Nash-Regret}(T)
be
\epsilon>0
and calculate the sample complexity
TK=O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{7}})
or
TK=O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{5}})
, respectively.
∎

Corollary 2

(Sample complexity for Markov cooperative games)
.

Assume the setting in
Theorem
4
except for Assumption
Assumption
2
. Suppose we compute
\widehat{w}_{i}^{(t)}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k\,=\,1}^{K}\beta_{k}^{(K)}{w}_{i}^{(k)}
via a stochastic projected gradient descent
(
38
)
with stepsize
\lambda^{(k)}=\frac{2}{2+k}
and
\beta_{k}^{(K)}=\frac{1/\lambda^{(k)}}{\sum_{r\,=\,1}^{K}1/\lambda^{(r)}}
. Then, if we choose stepsize
\eta=\frac{1-\gamma}{WNA\sqrt{T}}
and exploration rate
\xi=\min\left(\left(\frac{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}AN}{(1-\gamma)^{4}K}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},\frac{1}{2}\right)
, then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\,\text{Nash-Regret}(T)\,\right]\;\lesssim\;\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\rho}\,AN}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}\,\sqrt{T}}\,+\,\frac{W(\,{\kappa_{\rho}^{2}\,ANd}\,)^{\frac{1}{3}}}{(1-\gamma)^{\frac{7}{3}}\,K^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

Moreover, the sample complexity guarantee is
TK=O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{5}})
for obtaining an
\epsilon
-Nash equilibrium.

Proof of
Corollary
2
.

The proof follows the proof steps of
Corollary
1
above.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs for Section
6

In this section, we prove
Theorem
5
and
Theorem
6
in

Appendix
D.1
and
Appendix
D.2
, respectively.

D.1
Proof of
Theorem
5

It is convenient to introduce an auxiliary sequence
\{\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\}_{\tau\,=\,0}^{\infty}
associated with the learning rate
\{\alpha^{(t)}\}_{t\,=\,1}^{\infty}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\;\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\;\begin{cases}\displaystyle\prod_{j\,=\,1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)}),\quad&\text{for\ }\tau\,=\,0\\[5.69046pt]
\displaystyle\alpha^{(\tau)}\prod_{j\,=\,\tau+1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)}),\quad&\text{for\ }1\,\leq\,\tau\,\leq\,t\\[5.69046pt]
0,\quad&\text{for\ }\tau\,>\,t.\end{cases}

(26)

It is straightforward to verify that
\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}=1
for
t\geq 1
.

Lemma 8
.

In
Algorithm
3
,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}=\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}(x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(\tau)}y_{s}^{(\tau)}
for all
s,t
.

Proof of
Lemma
8
.

We prove it by induction. When
t=0
and
t=1
, it holds trivially by noting that
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(0)}=0
and
\alpha^{(1,1)}=\alpha^{(1)}
. Assume that it holds for
0,1,\ldots,t-1
. By the update rule for
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
3
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\;=\;(1-\alpha^{(t)}){\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}\,+\,\alpha^{(t)}(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}\;(1-\alpha^{(t)})\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}(x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(\tau)}y_{s}^{(\tau)}\,+\,\alpha^{(t,t)}(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{=}\;\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}(x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(\tau)}y_{s}^{(\tau)}

where
(a)
follows the induction hypothesis and
(b)
is due to the definition of
\alpha^{(t,\tau)}
.
∎

Lemma 9
.

In
Algorithm
3
, for every state
s
and time
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\;\geq\;\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{9}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

where
z_{s}^{(t)}=(x_{s}^{(t)},y_{s}^{(t)})
and
\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}=(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)},\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)})
.

Proof of
Lemma
9
.

We decompose the difference into three terms:

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\;\underbrace{(x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}}_{\textbf{Diff}_{x}}\,+\,\underbrace{(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}(y_{s}^{(t+1)}-y_{s}^{(t)})}_{\textbf{Diff}_{y}}\,+\,\underbrace{(x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}(y_{s}^{(t+1)}-y_{s}^{(t)})}_{\textbf{Diff}_{xy}}.

We next deal with
\textbf{Diff}_{x}
,
\textbf{Diff}_{y}
, and
\textbf{Diff}_{xy}
, separately.

Bounding
\textbf{Diff}_{x}
.
The optimality of
x_{s}^{(t+1)}
implies that for any
x_{s}^{\prime}\in\Delta({\mathcal{A}}_{1})
,

(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\;\geq\;(x_{s}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}

which implies that, by taking
x_{s}^{\prime}=\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}
,

(x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\;\geq\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}.

(27)

The optimality of
\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}
implies that for any
x_{s}^{\prime}\in\Delta({\mathcal{A}}_{1})
,

(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\;\geq\;(x_{s}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}

which implies that, by taking
x_{s}^{\prime}=x_{s}^{(t)}
,

(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\;\geq\;\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

(28)

Combining the two inequalities above yields

\textbf{Diff}_{x}\;=\;(x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\;\geq\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

(29)

Bounding
\textbf{Diff}_{y}
. Similarly,

\textbf{Diff}_{y}\;=\;(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}(y_{s}^{(t+1)}-y_{s}^{(t)})\;\geq\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|{y_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|{y_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

(30)

Bounding
\textbf{Diff}_{xy}
. By the AM-GM and Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities,

\displaystyle\textbf{Diff}_{xy}

\displaystyle\;\geq\;-\frac{\sqrt{A}}{2(1-\gamma)}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{\sqrt{A}}{2(1-\gamma)}\left\|{y_{s}^{(t+1)}-y_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\geq}\;-\frac{3\sqrt{A}}{2(1-\gamma)}\bigg(\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\,\left\|{y_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}-y_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\geq}\;-\frac{1}{16\eta}\bigg(\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\,\left\|{y_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{y}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\left\|{\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)}-y_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\bigg)

where
(a)
follows
\left\|{x+y+z}\right\|^{2}\leq 3\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|{z}\right\|^{2}
and
(b)
is by
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{32\sqrt{A}}
.

Finally, we complete the proof by summing up the bounds above for
\textbf{Diff}_{x}
,
\textbf{Diff}_{y}
, and
\textbf{Diff}_{xy}
.
∎

Lemma 10
.

In
Algorithm
3
, for all
t
and
s
, the following two inequalities hold:

(i)

\displaystyle\ \ {\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}\geq{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)};

(ii)

\displaystyle\ \ (x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\geq\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}-\frac{9}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

Proof of
Lemma
10
.

We first note that (ii) is a consequence of
Lemma
9
and (i),

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}\,-\,(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\;(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}\,-\,(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}\,+\,(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}\,-\,(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}\;\min_{s^{\prime}}\gamma\left({\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t-1)}\right)\,+\,\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{9}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}\;\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,-\,\frac{9}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

where
(a)
is due to
Lemma
9
, and the update of
{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
3
,

{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}(a_{1},a_{2})-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}(a_{1},a_{2})\;=\;\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\,\sim\,\mathbb{P}(\cdot\,|\,s,a_{1},a_{2})}\left[{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t-1)}\right]

and
(b)
follows (i).

Therefore, it suffices to prove (i). We prove it by induction.
Define
\zeta^{(t)}_{s}:=\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
and
\lambda^{(t)}_{s}:=\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
. For notational simplicity, define
{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(0)}=\mathbf{0}_{A\times A}
,
z_{s}^{(0)}=\bar{z}_{s}^{(0)}=\frac{1}{A}\boldsymbol{1}=z_{s}^{(1)}=\bar{z}_{s}^{(1)}
. Thus, (ii) holds for
t=0
and (i) holds for
t=1
. We note that for
t\geq 2
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\;\alpha^{(t)}\left(x^{(t)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y^{(t)}_{s}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\;\alpha^{(t)}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left(x^{(t)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y^{(t)}_{s}-x^{(\tau)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}y^{(\tau)}_{s}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;\alpha^{(t)}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\sum_{i\,=\,\tau}^{t-1}\left(x^{(i+1)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(i+1)}_{s}y^{(i+1)}_{s}-x^{(i)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(i)}_{s}y^{(i)}_{s}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;\alpha^{(t)}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\sum_{i\,=\,\tau}^{t-1}\left(x^{(i+1)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(i+1)}_{s}y^{(i+1)}_{s}-x^{(i)}_{s}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(i)}_{s}y^{(i)}_{s}-\frac{15}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i+1)}-\frac{7}{16\eta}\lambda_{s}^{(i)}+\frac{9}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha^{(t)}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\sum_{i\,=\,\tau}^{t-1}\left(\frac{15}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i+1)}+\frac{7}{16\eta}\lambda_{s}^{(i)}-\frac{9}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}\;\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{i\,=\,0}^{t-1}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\right)\left(\frac{15}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i+1)}-\frac{9}{16\eta}\zeta_{s}^{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle=\;\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{i\,=\,1}^{t}\zeta_{s}^{(i)}\left(\frac{15}{16\eta}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}-\frac{9}{16\eta}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\right)-\alpha^{(t)}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{0}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\right)\frac{9\eta}{16}\zeta_{s}^{(0)}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}\;\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{i\,=\,2}^{t}\zeta_{s}^{(i)}\left(\frac{15}{16\eta}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}-\frac{9}{16\eta}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\geq}\;0

where
(a)
follows the update of
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
in
Algorithm
3
, we apply
Lemma
8
and
\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}=1
in
(b)
,
(c)
follows the induction hypothesis (ii),
(d)
is due to that
\zeta_{s}^{(0)}=\zeta_{s}^{(1)}=0
, and we apply
Lemma
14
for
(e)
.
∎

Lemma 11
.

For every
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
, the following quantities in
Algorithm
3
all converge to some fixed values when
t\rightarrow\infty
:

(i)

{\mathcal{V}}^{(t)}_{s}
;

(ii)

\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t-1)}}\right\|^{2}
(converges to zero);

(iii)

(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}
.

Proof.

Establishing (i)
.
By (i) in
Lemma
10
,
\{{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}\}_{t\,=\,0}^{\infty}
is a bounded increasing sequence. By the monotone convergence theorem, it is convergent. Therefore, (i) holds.

Establishing (ii)
.
By summing up the inequality (ii) in
Lemma
10
over
t
and using the fact that
z_{s}^{(1)}=\bar{z}_{s}^{(1)}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\left(\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau+1)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau)}}\right\|^{2}\right)\;\leq\;(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(1)}y_{s}^{(1)}\;\leq\;\frac{1}{1-\gamma}

which implies that
\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau+1)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(\tau)}}\right\|^{2}
must converge to zero when
\tau\rightarrow\infty
, which further implies (ii).

Establishing (iii)
.
By (ii) in
Lemma
10
,

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}\,-\,\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\;\left((x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}-\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right)\,+\,\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\,+\,\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,\frac{15}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

converges to a fixed value (increasing and upper bounded). In (ii), we have shown that
\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
converges to zero. Therefore,
(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}
must also converge. Therefore, (iii) holds.
∎

Lemma 12
.

In
Algorithm
3
, for every
s\in{\mathcal{S}}
,
\lim_{t\,\rightarrow\,\infty}V^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}_{s}
exists, and

\lim_{t\,\rightarrow\,\infty}{\mathcal{V}}^{(t)}_{s}\;=\;\lim_{t\,\rightarrow\,\infty}V^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}_{s}.

Proof of
Lemma
12
.

By
Lemma
11
,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
and
(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}
both are convergent. Let
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}:=\lim_{t\,\rightarrow\,\infty}{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
and
\sigma_{s}^{\star}\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\lim_{t\,\rightarrow\,\infty}(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}
. We next show
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}=\sigma_{s}^{\star}
by contradiction. Assume that there exists
\epsilon>0
such that
|{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}|=\epsilon
. Since
(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}
converges to
\sigma_{s}^{\star}
, there exists some
t_{0}>0
such that for all
t\geq t_{0}
,

\displaystyle\left|(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}-\sigma^{\star}_{s}\right|\;\leq\;\frac{\epsilon}{3}.

(31)

By our choice of
\alpha^{(t)}
,
\sum_{t\,=\,t^{\prime}}^{\infty}\alpha^{(t)}=\infty
for any
t^{\prime}
. Thus, there exists
t_{1}>0
such that for all
t\geq t_{1}
and all
\tau\leq t_{0}
,

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\;\leq\;\prod_{i\,=\,\tau+1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(i)})\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;\exp\left(-\sum_{i\,=\,t_{0}+1}^{t}\alpha^{(i)}\right)\;\leq\;\frac{\epsilon(1-\gamma)}{3t_{0}}

(32)

where
\log(1-x)\leq-x
for
x\in(0,1)
is used in
(a)
.
By the update of
{\mathcal{V}}^{(t)}_{s}
in
Algorithm
3
,
for all
t\geq\max(t_{0},t_{1})
,

\displaystyle\left|{\mathcal{V}}^{(t)}_{s}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}\right|

\displaystyle\;=\;\left|\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\left((x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}y_{s}^{(\tau)}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;\left|\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t_{0}-1}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\left((x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}y_{s}^{(\tau)}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}\right)\right|\,+\,\left|\sum_{\tau\,=\,t_{0}}^{t}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\left((x_{s}^{(\tau)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}y_{s}^{(\tau)}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}\;\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t_{0}-1}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\right)\times\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\left(1-\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t_{0}-1}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\right)\times\frac{\epsilon}{3}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;t_{0}\max_{\tau\,\leq\,t_{0}}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\times\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\,+\,\frac{\epsilon}{3}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(c)}{\leq}\;\frac{2\epsilon}{3}

where we apply the triangle inequality for
(a)
,
(b)
is due to
(
31
)
and
\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}=1
, and
(c)
follows
(
32
)
. Since
\left|{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}-\sigma_{s}^{\star}\right|=\epsilon
, it is impossible that
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
converges to
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}
, and it must be that
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}=\sigma_{s}^{\star}
. Therefore,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}
converges to zero as
t\to\infty
.

Equivalently,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\left({\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}\right)\,+\,{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}-\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}x_{s}^{(t)}(a_{1})y_{s}^{(t)}(a_{2})\left(r(s,a_{1},a_{2})+\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\,\sim\,\mathbb{P}(\cdot\,|\,s,a_{1},a_{2})}\left[{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t-1)}\right]\right).

By letting
t\to 0
, since
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}\rightarrow 0
, thus,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}\,-\,\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}x_{s}^{(t)}(a_{1})y_{s}^{(t)}(a_{2})\left(r(s,a_{1},a_{2})+\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\,\sim\,\mathbb{P}(\cdot\,|\,s,a_{1},a_{2})}\left[{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t-1)}\right]\right)

also converges to zero. Hence,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t)}
converges to the unique fixed point of the Bellman equation. By the uniqueness,
{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}-V_{s}^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}
converges to zero. Therefore,
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}V_{s}^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}=\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}{\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{(t-1)}={\mathcal{V}}_{s}^{\star}
.
∎

Lemma 13
.

In
Algorithm
3
, for every
s
,

\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\max_{x^{\prime}}\;(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}\;=\;0.

Proof of
Lemma
13
.

By the optimality of
x_{s}^{(t+1)}
,

\big\langle x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t+1)},\,\eta{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}-x^{(t+1)}_{s}+\bar{x}^{(t+1)}_{s}\big\rangle\;\leq\;0,\;\text{ for any }x_{s}^{\prime}.

Rearranging the inequality yields, for any
x_{s}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\langle x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t+1)},{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\rangle

\displaystyle\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\left\langle x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t+1)},\,x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right\rangle\,+\,\left\langle x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)},\,\eta{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}-x^{(t+1)}_{s}+\bar{x}^{(t+1)}_{s}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|

\displaystyle\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\left\|{x_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|+\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}\,-\,x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\right).

By (ii) of
Lemma
11
, the right-hand side above converges to zero, which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 14
.

Let
\{\alpha^{(t)}\}_{t=1}^{\infty}
be a non-increasing sequence that satisfies
0<\alpha^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{6}
for all
t
. Then for any
t\geq i\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}\;\leq\;\frac{5}{3}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i-1}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}.

Proof of
Lemma
14
.

Equivalently, we prove

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t,i)}\;\leq\;\frac{2}{3}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{i-1}\alpha^{(t,\tau)}.

If suffices to show that
\alpha^{(t,i)}\leq\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,i-1)}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,i-2)}
.
We have the following two cases.

Case 1:
i>2
.
By the definition of
\alpha^{(t,\tau)}
and the monotonicity of
0<\alpha^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{6}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(t,i)}}{\alpha^{(t,i-1)}}

\displaystyle\;=\;\frac{\alpha^{(i)}\prod_{j\,=\,i+1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)})}{\alpha^{(i-1)}\prod_{j\,=\,i}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)})}\;=\;\frac{\alpha^{(i)}}{\alpha^{(i-1)}(1-\alpha^{(i)})}\;\leq\;\frac{1}{1-\alpha^{(i)}}\;\leq\;\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{6}}\;=\;\frac{6}{5}

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(t,i)}}{\alpha^{(t,i-2)}}

\displaystyle\;=\;\frac{\alpha^{(i)}}{\alpha^{(i-2)}(1-\alpha^{(i)})(1-\alpha^{(i-1)})}\;\leq\;\frac{36}{25}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,i-1)}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,i-2)}\;\geq\;\frac{2}{3}\left(\frac{5}{6}+\frac{25}{36}\right)\alpha^{(t,i)}\;\geq\;\alpha^{(t,i)}.

Case 2:
i=2
. By the definition of
\alpha^{(t,\tau)}
and the monotonicity of
0<\alpha^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{6}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(t,2)}}{\alpha^{(t,0)}}\;=\;\frac{\alpha^{(2)}\prod_{j\,=\,3}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)})}{\prod_{j\,=\,1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(j)})}\;=\;\frac{\alpha^{(2)}}{(1-\alpha^{(1)})(1-\alpha^{(2)})}\;\leq\;\frac{\frac{1}{6}}{\frac{5}{6}\times\frac{5}{6}}\;=\;\frac{6}{25}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,1)}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha^{(t,0)}\;\geq\;\frac{2}{3}\times\frac{25}{6}\alpha^{(t,2)}\;\geq\;\alpha^{(t,2)}.

∎

Proof of
Theorem
5
.

\displaystyle\max_{x^{\prime}}\left(\ V^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(\rho)-V^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)

\displaystyle=\;\max_{x^{\prime}}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}_{\rho}(s)\left(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}Q^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq\;\underbrace{\max_{x^{\prime}}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}_{\rho}(s)\left(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}y_{s}^{(t)}}_{\textbf{Diff}_{P}}+\underbrace{\max_{x^{\prime}}\ \frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}d^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}_{\rho}(s)\left(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}\left(Q^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}_{s}-{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}\right)y_{s}^{(t)}}_{\textbf{Diff}_{Q}}.

By
Lemma
13
,
\textbf{Diff}_{P}\rightarrow 0
when
t\rightarrow\infty
. For
\textbf{Diff}_{Q}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\left|Q_{s}^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}\right|\;\leq\;\gamma\max_{s^{\prime}}\left|V^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}_{s^{\prime}}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s^{\prime}}^{(t)}\right|

which converges to zero by
Lemma
12
. Therefore,
\textbf{Diff}_{Q}\rightarrow 0
when
t\rightarrow\infty
. Therefore,
(x^{(t)},y^{(t)})
converges to a Nash equilibrium when
t\rightarrow\infty
.
∎

D.2
Proof of
Theorem
6

We first introduce a corollary of
Lemma
10
.

Corollary 3
.

In
Algorithm
3
, for every state
s
, and any
T>0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\left(\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-z_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right)\;\leq\;\frac{8\eta}{1-\gamma}

where
z_{s}^{(t)}=(x_{s}^{(t)},y_{s}^{(t)})
and
\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}=(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)},\bar{y}_{s}^{(t)})
.

Proof of
Corollary
3
.

By (ii) of
Lemma
10
,

\displaystyle(x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}+\frac{15}{16\eta}\left(\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}-\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\;\frac{7}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\;\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{6}{16\eta}\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.

Thus, by the inequality
\left\|{x+y}\right\|^{2}\leq 2\left\|{x}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|{y}\right\|^{2}
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-z_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}&\leq&3\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}-z_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq&3\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|{\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}-z_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq&8\eta\left((x_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t+1)}y_{s}^{(t+1)}-(x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\right)\\
&&\displaystyle+\,\dfrac{15}{2}\left(\left\|{z_{s}^{(t)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}-\left\|{z_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{z}_{s}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}\right)\end{array}

which yields our desired result if we sum it over
t
, use
(x_{s}^{(T+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(T+1)}y_{s}^{(T+1)}\leq\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
and
z_{s}^{(1)}=\bar{z}_{s}^{(1)}
, and ignore a negative term.
∎

Lemma 15
.

In
Algorithm
3
, the gap between the critic
{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}
and the true
Q_{s}^{(t)}
satisfies

\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{s}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}-Q_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}_{\infty}\;\lesssim\;\frac{A}{(\alpha^{(T)})^{2}(1-\gamma)^{6}}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{s}\left(\left\|{x_{s}^{(t)}-x_{s}^{(t-1)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{y_{s}^{(t)}-y_{s}^{(t-1)}}\right\|^{2}\right).

Proof of
Lemma
15
.

For notational simplicity, define
{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(0)}=Q^{(0)}_{s}=\mathbf{0}_{A\times A}
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{s}\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\\
&\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=&\displaystyle\max_{s,a_{1},a_{2}}\left|Q^{(t)}_{s}(a_{1},a_{2})-{\mathcal{Q}}^{(t)}_{s}(a_{1},a_{2})\right|^{2}\\
&\leq&\displaystyle\max_{s,a_{1},a_{2}}\Bigg|r(s,a_{1},a_{2})+\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\,\sim\,\mathbb{P}(\cdot\,|\,s,a_{1},a_{2})}\left[(x_{s^{\prime}})^{\top}Q_{s^{\prime}}^{(t)}y_{s^{\prime}}\right]\\
&&\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\,\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left(r(s,a_{1},a_{2})+\gamma\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim\mathbb{P}(\cdot\,|\,s,a_{1},a_{2})}\left[(x^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}}y^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}}\right]\right)\Bigg|^{2}\\
&\leq&\displaystyle\gamma^{2}\max_{s^{\prime}}\left|\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left((x_{s^{\prime}})^{\top}Q_{s^{\prime}}^{(t)}y_{s^{\prime}}-(x^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}}y^{(\tau)}_{s^{\prime}}\right)\right|^{2}\\
&\overset{(a)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{6\gamma^{2}}{1-\gamma}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}(x^{(t)}_{s})^{\top}\left(Q^{(t)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}\right)y^{(t)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{1+\gamma}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}(x^{(t)}_{s})^{\top}\left(Q^{(\tau)}_{s}-{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}\right)y^{(t)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{6\gamma^{2}}{1-\gamma}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}(x^{(t)}_{s})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}(y^{(t)}_{s}-y^{(\tau)}_{s})\right)^{2}\\[5.69046pt]
&&\displaystyle+\,\frac{6\gamma^{2}}{1-\gamma}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}(x^{(t)}_{s}-x^{(\tau)}_{s})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}y^{(\tau)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\
&\overset{(b)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{1+\gamma}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\right)\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left((x^{(t)}_{s})^{\top}\left({\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}\right)y^{(t)}_{s}\right)^{2}\right)\\
&&\displaystyle+\,c^{\prime}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left(\|x^{(t)}_{s}-x^{(\tau)}_{s}\|_{1}+\|y^{(t)}_{s}-y^{(\tau)}_{s}\|_{1}\right)\right)^{2}\\
&\overset{(c)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{1+\gamma}\max_{s}\left[\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\right]\,+\,c^{\prime}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\sum_{h\,=\,\tau+1}^{t}\text{diff}^{(h)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\
&\leq&\displaystyle\gamma\max_{s}\left[\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\right]\,+\,c^{\prime}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{h\,=\,1}^{t}\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{h-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\text{diff}^{(h)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\
&\overset{(d)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\gamma\max_{s}\left[\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\right]\,+\,c^{\prime}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{h\,=\,1}^{t}\delta^{(t-1,h-1)}\text{diff}^{(h)}_{s}\right)^{2}\\
&\overset{(e)}{\leq}&\displaystyle\gamma\max_{s}\left[\sum_{\tau\,=\,0}^{t-1}\alpha^{(t-1,\tau)}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}^{(\tau)}_{s}-Q^{(\tau)}_{s}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\right]\,+\,c^{\prime}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{h\,=\,1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(t)})^{t-h}\text{diff}^{(h)}_{s}\right)^{2}\end{array}

where in
(a)
we apply
(x+y+z+w)^{2}\leq\frac{6x^{2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2y^{2}}{1+\gamma}+\frac{6z^{2}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{6w^{2}}{1-\gamma}
from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, in
(b)
we use Lemma
17
and obtain
c^{\prime}=O\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\right)
, in
(c)
we introduce notation,

\text{diff}_{s}^{(h)}\;\mathrel{\mathop{:}}=\;\left\|{x^{(h)}_{s}-x^{(h-1)}_{s}}\right\|_{1}+\left\|{y^{(h)}_{s}-y^{(h-1)}_{s}}\right\|_{1}

and in
(d)
we introduce notation,

\displaystyle\delta^{(t,\tau)}\;=\;\prod_{i\,=\,\tau+1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(i)})

and apply Lemma
35
of
Wei et al. 2021b
,
(e)
is due to that
\{\alpha^{(t)}\}_{t\,=\,0}^{\infty}
is a non-increasing sequence.

Application of Lemma 33 of
Wei et al. 2021b
to the recursion relation above yields

\displaystyle\max_{s}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}-Q^{(t)}_{s}}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;c^{\prime}\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\beta^{(t,\tau)}\max_{s}\left(\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}\text{diff}_{s}^{(q)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;c^{\prime}\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\beta^{(t,\tau)}\left(\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}\text{diff}^{(q)}\right)^{2}

(33)

where
\beta^{(t,\tau)}:=\alpha^{(\tau)}\prod_{i=\tau}^{t-1}(1-\alpha^{(i)}+\alpha^{(i)}\gamma)
for
1\leq\tau<t
and
\beta^{(t,t)}:=1
, and
\text{diff}^{(t)}:=\max_{s}\text{diff}^{(t)}_{s}
.

The right-hand side of
(
33
)
can be further upper bounded by

\displaystyle c^{\prime}\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\beta^{(t,\tau)}\left(\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}\right)\left(\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;c^{\prime}\sum_{\tau\,=\,1}^{t}\frac{\beta^{(t,\tau)}}{\alpha^{(\tau)}}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\;c^{\prime}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}\sum_{\tau\,=\,q}^{t}\frac{\beta^{(t,\tau)}}{\alpha^{(\tau)}}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\;c^{\prime}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}\left[\sum_{\tau\,=\,q}^{t-1}(1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma)^{t-\tau}(1-\alpha^{(t)})^{\tau-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}+\frac{(1-\alpha^{(t)})^{t-q}}{\alpha^{(t)}}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\;c^{\prime}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}\left[(1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma)^{t-q}\sum_{\tau\,=\,q}^{t-1}\left(\frac{1-\alpha^{(t)}}{1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma}\right)^{\tau-q}+\frac{(1-\alpha^{(t)})^{t-q}}{\alpha^{(t)}}\right](\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\;c^{\prime}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}\left[(1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma)^{t-q}\frac{1-\left(\frac{1-\alpha^{(t)}}{1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma}\right)^{t-q}}{\frac{\alpha^{(t)}\gamma}{1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma}}+\frac{(1-\alpha^{(t)})^{t-q}}{\alpha^{(t)}}\right](\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\;\frac{2c^{\prime}}{\alpha^{(t)}\gamma}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma)^{t-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

where
(a)
is due to that
\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{\tau}(1-\alpha^{(\tau)})^{\tau-q}\leq\frac{1}{\alpha^{(\tau)}}.

Substitution of the upper bound above into
(
33
)
yields,

\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{s}\left\|{{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}-Q_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\;\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\frac{c^{\prime}}{\alpha^{(t)}}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{t}(1-\alpha^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\gamma)^{t-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}\;\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{t\,=\,q}^{T}\frac{c^{\prime}}{\alpha^{(T)}}(1-\alpha^{(T)}+\alpha^{(T)}\gamma)^{t-q}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}\;\frac{c^{\prime}}{(\alpha^{(T)})^{2}(1-\gamma)}\sum_{q\,=\,1}^{T}(\text{diff}^{(q)})^{2}

where
(a)
is due to that
\alpha^{(t)}
is non-increasing, and
(b)
is due to that
\sum_{t\,=\,q}^{T}(1-\alpha^{(T)}+\alpha^{(T)}\gamma)^{t-q}\leq\frac{1}{\alpha^{(T)}(1-\gamma)}
.

Finally, using the definition of
c^{\prime}
and applying
\|\cdot\|_{1}\leq\sqrt{A}\|\cdot\|
to
\text{diff}^{(q)}
lead to the desired result.
∎

Proof of
Theorem
6
.

The proof consists of two parts: Markov cooperative games and Markov competitive games, separately.

Markov cooperative games
.
Fix
s
, the optimality of
\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}
in
Algorithm
3
yields

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\big\langle\eta{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}-(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}),x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}\big\rangle&\leq&0,\;\text{ for any }x_{s}^{\prime}\in\Delta({\mathcal{A}}_{1}).\end{array}

(34)

Thus, for any
x_{s}^{\prime}\in\Delta({\mathcal{A}}_{1})
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}Q_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\\[5.69046pt]
&=&\displaystyle(x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\,+\,(x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}(Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)})y_{s}^{(t)}\,+\,(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)})^{\top}Q_{s}^{(t)}y_{s}^{(t)}\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}(x_{s}^{\prime}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)})^{\top}(\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)})\,+\,\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\,+\,\frac{\sqrt{A}}{1-\gamma}\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\\[5.69046pt]
&\overset{(b)}{\lesssim}&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\,+\,\left\|{\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\right)\,+\,\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\end{array}

where we use
(
34
)
and
\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{A}}{1-\gamma}
in
(a)
, and
(b)
is due to the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the choice of
\eta\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{32\sqrt{A}}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\left(\max_{x^{\prime}}V^{x^{\prime},\,y^{(t)}}(\rho)-V^{x^{(t)},y^{(t)}}(\rho)\right)

\displaystyle=\;\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\max_{x^{\prime}}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left(x_{s}^{\prime}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}Q_{s}^{{(t)}}y_{s}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left(\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}\right\|+\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right\|\right)\,+\,\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\lesssim}\;\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left(\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\,\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\;\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\times\,\sqrt{\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}\left(\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-\bar{x}_{s}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\bar{x}_{s}^{(t+1)}-x_{s}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right)}\,+\,\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)}\sum_{t\,=\,1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\lesssim}\;\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\eta(1-\gamma)}\sqrt{\frac{\eta S}{1-\gamma}}\,+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s}d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s}\left\|{Q_{s}^{(t)}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{s}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\lesssim}\;\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{ST}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{3}}}\,+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\frac{SA}{(\alpha^{(T)})^{2}(1-\gamma)^{6}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\max_{s}\left(\left\|{x_{s}^{(t)}-x_{s}^{(t-1)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{y_{s}^{(t)}-y_{s}^{(t-1)}}\right\|^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\lesssim}\;\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{ST}{\eta(1-\gamma)^{3}}}\,+\,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\frac{\eta S^{2}A}{{(\alpha^{(T)})}^{2}(1-\gamma)^{7}}}

where we apply the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for
(a)
,
(b)
follows the state distribution
d_{\rho}^{x^{\prime},y^{(t)}}(s)
,
(c)
is due to
Corollary
3
,
(d)
is because of
Lemma
15
, and
(e)
again is due to
Corollary
3
.
By taking
\eta=\frac{(1-\gamma)^{2}}{32\sqrt{SA}}
and
\alpha^{(t)}=\frac{1}{6\sqrt[3]{t}}
, the last upper bound above is of order,
